import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { dispatchOutboxUseCase } from "../../../src/application/outbox/dispatch-outbox.js";
import type { EventPublisher } from "../../../src/application/ports/event-publisher.js";
import type { DomainEvent } from "../../../src/domain/shared/domain-event.js";
import {
  createTestEnvironment,
  FIXED_NOW,
  registerTestCustomer,
} from "../../support/test-environment.js";

async function stageCustomerEvent(
  environment: ReturnType<typeof createTestEnvironment>,
) {
  return registerTestCustomer(environment, {
    email: "linus@example.com",
    name: "Linus",
  });
}

function failingPublisher(): EventPublisher {
  return {
    async publish() {
      throw new Error("broker unavailable");
    },
  };
}

function observableClock() {
  let now = FIXED_NOW;

  return {
    clock: { now: () => now },
    advance(ms: number) {
      now = new Date(new Date(now).getTime() + ms).toISOString();
    },
  };
}

describe("outbox dispatcher", () => {
  it("publishes due messages once and marks them delivered", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    await stageCustomerEvent(environment);

    const pendingBefore = await environment.repositories.outbox.findPending(
      10,
      FIXED_NOW,
    );
    expect(pendingBefore).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(pendingBefore[0]?.attempts).toBe(0);
    expect(pendingBefore[0]?.nextAttemptAt).toBe(FIXED_NOW);

    const result = await environment.useCases.dispatchOutbox();

    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a dispatch");
    expect(result.value).toEqual({
      published: 1,
      failed: 0,
      deadLettered: 0,
    });
    expect(environment.published.map((event) => event.name)).toEqual([
      "customer.registered",
    ]);

    await environment.useCases.dispatchOutbox();
    expect(environment.published).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("schedules a retry with backoff instead of retrying immediately", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    await stageCustomerEvent(environment);

    const { clock, advance } = observableClock();
    const dispatch = dispatchOutboxUseCase({
      outbox: environment.repositories.outbox,
      publisher: failingPublisher(),
      clock,
    });

    const failed = await dispatch();

    expect(failed.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!failed.ok) throw new Error("Expected a summary");
    expect(failed.value).toEqual({ published: 0, failed: 1, deadLettered: 0 });

    // Not due yet: the backoff has not elapsed.
    expect(
      await environment.repositories.outbox.findPending(10, clock.now()),
    ).toEqual([]);

    // The message is still pending, with one attempt recorded.
    const [message] = await environment.repositories.outbox.findPending(
      10,
      "2026-01-01T00:00:01.000Z",
    );
    expect(message).toMatchObject({
      attempts: 1,
      lastError: "broker unavailable",
      nextAttemptAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:01.000Z",
      deadLetteredAt: null,
    });

    advance(1_000);

    const recovered = dispatchOutboxUseCase({
      outbox: environment.repositories.outbox,
      publisher: {
        async publish(event) {
          environment.published.push(event);
        },
      },
      clock,
    });

    expect(await recovered()).toMatchObject({
      ok: true,
      value: { published: 1, failed: 0, deadLettered: 0 },
    });
  });

  it("dead-letters a message after the attempt budget", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    await stageCustomerEvent(environment);

    const { clock, advance } = observableClock();
    const dispatch = dispatchOutboxUseCase({
      outbox: environment.repositories.outbox,
      publisher: failingPublisher(),
      clock,
    });

    // Five failed deliveries, each after its backoff window.
    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 5; attempt += 1) {
      const result = await dispatch();
      expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
      advance(60_000);
    }

    const dead = await environment.repositories.outbox.findDeadLettered(10);
    expect(dead).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(dead[0]).toMatchObject({
      attempts: 5,
      lastError: "broker unavailable",
    });

    // Dead letters are never picked up again.
    expect(
      await environment.repositories.outbox.findPending(10, clock.now()),
    ).toEqual([]);

    const after = await dispatch();
    expect(after).toMatchObject({
      ok: true,
      value: { published: 0, failed: 0, deadLettered: 0 },
    });
  });

  it("keeps the payload broker-agnostic", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    await stageCustomerEvent(environment);

    const [message] = await environment.repositories.outbox.findPending(
      10,
      FIXED_NOW,
    );

    expect(message?.event).toEqual<Partial<DomainEvent>>({
      name: "customer.registered",
      occurredAt: expect.any(String) as unknown as string,
      payload: expect.objectContaining({
        customerId: expect.any(String) as unknown as string,
      }) as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,
    });

    expect(JSON.stringify(message)).not.toContain("topic");
    expect(JSON.stringify(message)).not.toContain("kafka");
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  assignRole,
  createAccount,
  MAX_PASSWORD_LENGTH,
  MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH,
  validatePassword,
  type Account,
} from "../../../src/domain/auth/account.js";
import type { Role } from "../../../src/domain/auth/role.js";

const NOW = "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z";

function buildAccount(): Account {
  const result = createAccount({
    customerId: "customer-1",
    passwordHash: "scrypt$1024$8$1$c2FsdA$aGFzaA",
    role: "customer",
    createdAt: NOW,
  });

  if (!result.ok) throw new Error(result.error.message);
  return result.value;
}

describe("password policy", () => {
  it("accepts a passphrase at the minimum length", () => {
    const result = validatePassword("a".repeat(MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH));

    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it("rejects a password below the minimum", () => {
    const result = validatePassword("a".repeat(MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH - 1));

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a rejection");
    expect(result.error.type).toBe("invalid_password");
    expect(result.error.message).toContain(String(MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH));
  });

  it("rejects a password above the maximum", () => {
    const result = validatePassword("a".repeat(MAX_PASSWORD_LENGTH + 1));

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a rejection");
    expect(result.error.message).toContain(String(MAX_PASSWORD_LENGTH));
  });
});

describe("account", () => {
  it("cannot exist without a credential hash", () => {
    const result = createAccount({
      customerId: "customer-1",
      passwordHash: "   ",
      role: "customer",
      createdAt: NOW,
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a rejection");
    expect(result.error.type).toBe("invalid_password");
  });

  it("starts as a customer and can be promoted", () => {
    const account = buildAccount();
    expect(account.role).toBe("customer");

    const promoted = assignRole(account, "admin", NOW);

    expect(promoted.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!promoted.ok) throw new Error("Expected a promotion");
    expect(promoted.value.role).toBe("admin");
    expect(promoted.value.passwordHash).toBe(account.passwordHash);
  });

  it("rejects an unknown role even at runtime", () => {
    const result = createAccount({
      customerId: "customer-1",
      passwordHash: "hash",
      role: "root" as Role,
      createdAt: NOW,
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a rejection");
    expect(result.error.type).toBe("invalid_role");
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  addItem,
  cartItemCount,
  cartTotal,
  createCart,
  removeItem,
} from "../../../src/domain/cart/cart.js";
import { MAX_ITEM_QUANTITY } from "../../../src/domain/cart/cart-item.js";
import { createMoney } from "../../../src/domain/product/money.js";

const addedAt = "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z";

function usd(amountInMinorUnits: number) {
  const money = createMoney(amountInMinorUnits, "usd");

  if (!money.ok) {
    throw new Error("Expected valid money");
  }

  return money.value;
}

function emptyCart() {
  return createCart({
    id: "cart-1",
    customerId: "customer-1",
    createdAt: addedAt,
  });
}

describe("cart", () => {
  it("adds a line and derives the total from the lines", () => {
    const cart = addItem(emptyCart(), {
      productId: "p1",
      quantity: 2,
      unitPrice: usd(1999),
      addedAt,
    });

    expect(cart.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!cart.ok) throw new Error("Expected a cart");

    expect(cartItemCount(cart.value)).toBe(2);
    expect(cartTotal(cart.value)).toEqual({
      amountInMinorUnits: 3998,
      currency: "USD",
    });
  });

  it("merges a repeated product instead of adding a second line", () => {
    const first = addItem(emptyCart(), {
      productId: "p1",
      quantity: 1,
      unitPrice: usd(500),
      addedAt,
    });

    if (!first.ok) throw new Error("Expected a cart");

    const second = addItem(first.value, {
      productId: "p1",
      quantity: 2,
      unitPrice: usd(500),
      addedAt,
    });

    if (!second.ok) throw new Error("Expected a cart");

    expect(second.value.items).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(second.value.items[0]?.quantity).toBe(3);
  });

  it("rejects a quantity above the per-line limit", () => {
    const result = addItem(emptyCart(), {
      productId: "p1",
      quantity: MAX_ITEM_QUANTITY + 1,
      unitPrice: usd(100),
      addedAt,
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");

    expect(result.error.type).toBe("invalid_cart_quantity");
  });

  it("rejects a second currency in the same cart", () => {
    const first = addItem(emptyCart(), {
      productId: "p1",
      quantity: 1,
      unitPrice: usd(100),
      addedAt,
    });

    if (!first.ok) throw new Error("Expected a cart");

    const euros = createMoney(100, "eur");
    if (!euros.ok) throw new Error("Expected valid money");

    const second = addItem(first.value, {
      productId: "p2",
      quantity: 1,
      unitPrice: euros.value,
      addedAt,
    });

    expect(second.ok).toBe(false);
    if (second.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");

    expect(second.error.type).toBe("cart_currency_mismatch");
  });

  it("removes a line and refuses to remove a missing one", () => {
    const added = addItem(emptyCart(), {
      productId: "p1",
      quantity: 1,
      unitPrice: usd(100),
      addedAt,
    });

    if (!added.ok) throw new Error("Expected a cart");

    const removed = removeItem(added.value, "p1", addedAt);
    if (!removed.ok) throw new Error("Expected a cart");

    expect(removed.value.items).toHaveLength(0);

    const missing = removeItem(removed.value, "p1", addedAt);

    expect(missing.ok).toBe(false);
    if (missing.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");

    expect(missing.error.type).toBe("item_not_in_cart");
  });

  it("has no total while empty", () => {
    expect(cartTotal(emptyCart())).toBeNull();
  });
});

FILE: ecommerce-backend/tests/unit/domain/inventory-availability.test.ts
65 lines | 2,009 bytes

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  checkAvailability,
  createInventoryItem,
  reserveStock,
} from "../../../src/domain/inventory/inventory-item.js";

function stock(quantity: number) {
  const item = createInventoryItem({
    id: "inv-1",
    productId: "prod-1",
    quantity,
    updatedAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
  });

  if (!item.ok) throw new Error("Expected stock");
  return item.value;
}

describe("checkAvailability", () => {
  it("accepts a request the free stock can cover", () => {
    expect(checkAvailability(stock(3), 3)).toEqual({ ok: true, value: true });
  });

  it("rejects a request above the free stock", () => {
    const result = checkAvailability(stock(1), 2);

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");
    expect(result.error.type).toBe("insufficient_stock");
  });

  it("rejects a nonsensical quantity", () => {
    const result = checkAvailability(stock(5), 0);

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");
    expect(result.error.type).toBe("invalid_quantity");
  });

  it("never mutates the item, unlike a reservation", () => {
    const item = stock(5);

    checkAvailability(item, 4);

    expect(item).toMatchObject({ available: 5, reserved: 0 });

    const reserved = reserveStock(item, 4, "2026-01-02T00:00:00.000Z");
    expect(reserved.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!reserved.ok) throw new Error("Expected a reservation");
    expect(reserved.value).toMatchObject({ available: 1, reserved: 4 });
    expect(item).toMatchObject({ available: 5, reserved: 0 });
  });

  it("is the single rule reserveStock applies", () => {
    const result = reserveStock(stock(1), 2, "2026-01-02T00:00:00.000Z");

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");
    expect(result.error).toEqual({
      type: "insufficient_stock",
      message: "Only 1 unit(s) of product prod-1 are available",
    });
  });
});

FILE: ecommerce-backend/tests/unit/domain/inventory.test.ts
76 lines | 2,200 bytes
================================================================================
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  commitReservation,
  createInventoryItem,
  releaseStock,
  reserveStock,
} from "../../../src/domain/inventory/inventory-item.js";

const at = "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z";

function stockOf(quantity: number) {
  const item = createInventoryItem({
    id: "inventory-1",
    productId: "p1",
    quantity,
    updatedAt: at,
  });

  if (!item.ok) {
    throw new Error("Expected an inventory item");
  }

  return item.value;
}

describe("inventory", () => {
  it("moves units from available to reserved when stock is held", () => {
    const reserved = reserveStock(stockOf(3), 2, at);

    expect(reserved.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!reserved.ok) throw new Error("Expected a reservation");

    expect(reserved.value.available).toBe(1);
    expect(reserved.value.reserved).toBe(2);
  });

  it("refuses to reserve more than is available", () => {
    const reserved = reserveStock(stockOf(1), 2, at);

    expect(reserved.ok).toBe(false);
    if (reserved.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");

    expect(reserved.error.type).toBe("insufficient_stock");
  });

  it("returns held units to the free pool on release", () => {
    const reserved = reserveStock(stockOf(3), 2, at);
    if (!reserved.ok) throw new Error("Expected a reservation");

    const released = releaseStock(reserved.value, 2, at);
    if (!released.ok) throw new Error("Expected a release");

    expect(released.value.available).toBe(3);
    expect(released.value.reserved).toBe(0);
  });

  it("consumes held units on commit without freeing them", () => {
    const reserved = reserveStock(stockOf(3), 2, at);
    if (!reserved.ok) throw new Error("Expected a reservation");

    const committed = commitReservation(reserved.value, 2, at);
    if (!committed.ok) throw new Error("Expected a commit");

    expect(committed.value.available).toBe(1);
    expect(committed.value.reserved).toBe(0);
  });

  it("rejects a non-integer quantity", () => {
    const result = reserveStock(stockOf(3), 1.5, at);

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");

    expect(result.error.type).toBe("invalid_quantity");
  });
});

FILE: ecommerce-backend/tests/unit/domain/order.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  cancelOrder,
  createOrder,
  payOrder,
} from "../../../src/domain/order/order.js";
import { createOrderItem } from "../../../src/domain/order/order-item.js";
import { createMoney } from "../../../src/domain/product/money.js";

const at = "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z";

function usd(amountInMinorUnits: number) {
  const money = createMoney(amountInMinorUnits, "usd");

  if (!money.ok) {
    throw new Error("Expected valid money");
  }

  return money.value;
}

function item(productId: string, quantity: number, unitPrice: number) {
  const created = createOrderItem({
    productId,
    quantity,
    unitPrice: usd(unitPrice),
  });

  if (!created.ok) {
    throw new Error("Expected an order item");
  }

  return created.value;
}

function pendingOrder() {
  const order = createOrder({
    id: "order-1",
    customerId: "customer-1",
    items: [item("p1", 2, 1000), item("p2", 1, 500)],
    createdAt: at,
  });

  if (!order.ok) {
    throw new Error("Expected an order");
  }

  return order.value;
}

describe("order", () => {
  it("derives its total from the lines", () => {
    expect(pendingOrder().total).toEqual({
      amountInMinorUnits: 2500,
      currency: "USD",
    });
  });

  it("starts pending payment", () => {
    expect(pendingOrder().status).toBe("pending_payment");
  });

  it("rejects an order without lines", () => {
    const order = createOrder({
      id: "order-1",
      customerId: "customer-1",
      items: [],
      createdAt: at,
    });

    expect(order.ok).toBe(false);
    if (order.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");

    expect(order.error.type).toBe("empty_order");
  });

  it("marks an order paid exactly once", () => {
    const paid = payOrder(pendingOrder(), at);
    if (!paid.ok) throw new Error("Expected a paid order");

    expect(paid.value.status).toBe("paid");
    expect(paid.value.paidAt).toBe(at);

    const again = payOrder(paid.value, at);

    expect(again.ok).toBe(false);
    if (again.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");

    expect(again.error.type).toBe("order_already_paid");
  });

  it("cancels a pending order and refuses to cancel a paid one", () => {
    const cancelled = cancelOrder(pendingOrder(), at);
    if (!cancelled.ok) throw new Error("Expected a cancelled order");

    expect(cancelled.value.status).toBe("cancelled");

    const paid = payOrder(pendingOrder(), at);
    if (!paid.ok) throw new Error("Expected a paid order");

    const result = cancelOrder(paid.value, at);

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");

    expect(result.error.type).toBe("order_already_paid");
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createInMemoryIdempotencyStore } from "../../../src/infrastructure/idempotency/in-memory-idempotency-store.js";

describe("in-memory idempotency store", () => {
  it("lets exactly one caller claim a key", async () => {
    const store = createInMemoryIdempotencyStore();

    expect(await store.acquire("checkout:a:1", 30)).toBe("acquired");
    expect(await store.acquire("checkout:a:1", 30)).toBe("busy");
  });

  it("stores an outcome and frees the claim", async () => {
    const store = createInMemoryIdempotencyStore();

    await store.acquire("key", 30);
    await store.save(
      "key",
      { result: { id: "order-1" }, createdAt: "now" },
      60,
    );

    expect(await store.find("key")).toEqual({
      result: { id: "order-1" },
      createdAt: "now",
    });
    expect(await store.acquire("key", 30)).toBe("acquired");
  });

  it("releases a claim so a failed attempt can be retried", async () => {
    const store = createInMemoryIdempotencyStore();

    await store.acquire("key", 30);
    await store.release("key");

    expect(await store.acquire("key", 30)).toBe("acquired");
  });

  it("expires claims and records on their own", async () => {
    let clock = 0;
    const store = createInMemoryIdempotencyStore(() => clock);

    await store.acquire("key", 1);
    await store.save("key", { result: 1, createdAt: "now" }, 1);

    clock = 1_500;

    expect(await store.acquire("key", 1)).toBe("acquired");
    expect(await store.find("key")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("returns copies so stored outcomes cannot be mutated", async () => {
    const store = createInMemoryIdempotencyStore();
    const record = { result: { id: "order-1" }, createdAt: "now" };

    await store.save("key", record, 60);
    const found = await store.find<{ id: string }>("key");

    if (found === null) throw new Error("Expected a record");
    found.result.id = "changed";

    expect(await store.find("key")).toMatchObject({
      result: { id: "order-1" },
    });
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createInMemoryInboxStore } from "../../../src/infrastructure/inbox/in-memory-inbox-store.js";

describe("inbox", () => {
  it("accepts an event once and reports the redelivery as seen", async () => {
    const inbox = createInMemoryInboxStore();
    const input = {
      id: "event-1",
      eventName: "order.paid",
      processedAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
    };

    expect(await inbox.markProcessed(input)).toBe(true);
    expect(await inbox.markProcessed(input)).toBe(false);
    expect(await inbox.hasProcessed("event-1")).toBe(true);
    expect(await inbox.hasProcessed("event-2")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("treats different events independently", async () => {
    const inbox = createInMemoryInboxStore();

    expect(
      await inbox.markProcessed({
        id: "a",
        eventName: "order.created",
        processedAt: "now",
      }),
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(
      await inbox.markProcessed({
        id: "b",
        eventName: "order.created",
        processedAt: "now",
      }),
    ).toBe(true);
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { DomainEvent } from "../../../src/domain/shared/domain-event.js";
import {
  DEFAULT_TOPIC,
  partitionKeyForEvent,
  toKafkaMessage,
  topicForEvent,
} from "../../../src/infrastructure/kafka/kafka-messages.js";

const orderEvent: DomainEvent = {
  name: "order.paid",
  occurredAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
  payload: {
    orderId: "order-1",
    customerId: "customer-1",
    providerReference: "test_1",
  },
};

describe("kafka message mapping", () => {
  it("routes known events to their topic", () => {
    expect(topicForEvent("order.created")).toBe("ecommerce.orders.v1");
    expect(topicForEvent("order.paid")).toBe("ecommerce.orders.v1");
    expect(topicForEvent("order.cancelled")).toBe("ecommerce.orders.v1");
    expect(topicForEvent("customer.registered")).toBe("ecommerce.customers.v1");
  });

  it("falls back to the catch-all topic for unknown events", () => {
    expect(topicForEvent("something.new")).toBe(DEFAULT_TOPIC);
  });

  it("partitions by order id, then customer id", () => {
    expect(partitionKeyForEvent(orderEvent)).toBe("order-1");
    expect(
      partitionKeyForEvent({
        name: "customer.registered",
        occurredAt: "now",
        payload: { customerId: "customer-1" },
      }),
    ).toBe("customer-1");
    expect(
      partitionKeyForEvent({ name: "odd", occurredAt: "now", payload: {} }),
    ).toBeNull();
  });

  it("carries the outbox id as body id and header, for consumer dedupe", () => {
    const message = toKafkaMessage(orderEvent, { id: "outbox-1" });

    expect(message).toMatchObject({
      topic: "ecommerce.orders.v1",
      key: "order-1",
      headers: {
        "x-event-id": "outbox-1",
        "x-event-name": "order.paid",
        "x-event-version": "1",
      },
    });
    expect(JSON.parse(message.value)).toMatchObject({
      id: "outbox-1",
      name: "order.paid",
      payload: { orderId: "order-1" },
    });
  });

  it("never leaks a topic name into the event body", () => {
    const message = toKafkaMessage(orderEvent, { id: "outbox-1" });

    expect(JSON.parse(message.value)).not.toHaveProperty("topic");
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createInMemoryRateLimiter } from "../../../src/infrastructure/rate-limit/in-memory-rate-limiter.js";

describe("in-memory rate limiter", () => {
  it("allows the limit and rejects the next request", async () => {
    const limiter = createInMemoryRateLimiter({
      limit: 2,
      windowSeconds: 60,
    });

    expect(await limiter.consume("ip:/checkout")).toMatchObject({
      allowed: true,
      remaining: 1,
    });
    expect(await limiter.consume("ip:/checkout")).toMatchObject({
      allowed: true,
      remaining: 0,
    });
    expect(await limiter.consume("ip:/checkout")).toMatchObject({
      allowed: false,
      remaining: 0,
    });
  });

  it("counts callers independently", async () => {
    const limiter = createInMemoryRateLimiter({ limit: 1, windowSeconds: 60 });

    expect((await limiter.consume("a:/checkout")).allowed).toBe(true);
    expect((await limiter.consume("b:/checkout")).allowed).toBe(true);
    expect((await limiter.consume("a:/checkout")).allowed).toBe(false);
  });

  it("resets when the window elapses", async () => {
    let clock = 1_000;
    const limiter = createInMemoryRateLimiter({
      limit: 1,
      windowSeconds: 10,
      now: () => clock,
    });

    expect((await limiter.consume("ip")).allowed).toBe(true);
    expect((await limiter.consume("ip")).allowed).toBe(false);

    clock += 10_000;

    expect((await limiter.consume("ip")).allowed).toBe(true);
  });
});

FILE: ecommerce-backend/tests/unit/infrastructure/unit-of-work.test.ts
114 lines | 4,041 bytes

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  buildInMemoryDependencies,
  buildUseCases,
} from "../../../src/main/container.js";
import { err, ok } from "../../../src/domain/shared/result.js";
import { createMoney } from "../../../src/domain/product/money.js";
import { createProduct } from "../../../src/domain/product/product.js";
import { createInMemoryStore } from "../../../src/infrastructure/persistence/in-memory/store.js";
import { createInMemoryProductRepository } from "../../../src/infrastructure/persistence/repositories/in-memory-product-repository.js";
import { createInMemoryUnitOfWork } from "../../../src/infrastructure/transactions/in-memory-unit-of-work.js";
import { createTestAuth } from "../../support/test-environment.js";

function sampleProduct(id: string) {
  const price = createMoney(1999, "usd");
  if (!price.ok) throw new Error("Expected money");

  const product = createProduct({
    id,
    name: "Keyboard",
    price: price.value,
    createdAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
  });
  if (!product.ok) throw new Error("Expected a product");

  return product.value;
}

describe("in-memory unit of work", () => {
  it("commits every write when the work succeeds", async () => {
    const store = createInMemoryStore();
    const products = createInMemoryProductRepository(store);
    const unitOfWork = createInMemoryUnitOfWork(store);

    const result = await unitOfWork.execute(async () => {
      await products.save(sampleProduct("p-1"));
      await products.save(sampleProduct("p-2"));
      return ok("done");
    });

    expect(result).toEqual(ok("done"));
    expect(await products.findById("p-1")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(await products.findById("p-2")).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("rolls back every write when the work returns a failure", async () => {
    const store = createInMemoryStore();
    const products = createInMemoryProductRepository(store);
    const unitOfWork = createInMemoryUnitOfWork(store);

    const result = await unitOfWork.execute(async () => {
      await products.save(sampleProduct("p-1"));
      return err({ type: "business_failure", message: "nope" });
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");
    expect(result.error.type).toBe("business_failure");
    expect(await products.findById("p-1")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("rolls back and reports transaction_failed when the work throws", async () => {
    const store = createInMemoryStore();
    const products = createInMemoryProductRepository(store);
    const unitOfWork = createInMemoryUnitOfWork(store);

    const result = await unitOfWork.execute(async () => {
      await products.save(sampleProduct("p-1"));
      throw new Error("connection reset");
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");
    expect(result.error).toEqual({
      type: "transaction_failed",
      message: "connection reset",
    });
    expect(await products.findById("p-1")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("rolls back a real use case when a later write fails", async () => {
    const dependencies = buildInMemoryDependencies();
    const failingInventory = {
      ...dependencies.inventory,
      async save(): Promise<void> {
        throw new Error("disk full");
      },
    };

    const useCases = buildUseCases(
      {
        ...dependencies,
        inventory: failingInventory,
      },
      createTestAuth(),
    );

    const created = await useCases.createProduct({
      name: "Keyboard",
      amountInMinorUnits: 1999,
      currency: "usd",
      availableQuantity: 5,
    });

    expect(created.ok).toBe(false);
    if (created.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");
    expect(created.error.type).toBe("transaction_failed");

    // The product row was written first, but the transaction undid it.
    const products = await dependencies.products.findAll();
    expect(products).toEqual([]);
    expect(await dependencies.inventory.findByProductId("id-1")).toBeNull();
  });
});
